                      <TextBlock Text="Consulta manual via journalctl, sem privilégios administrativos. Mostra até 500 registros recentes do kernel, prioridades 0 a 4 (emergência a aviso), da inicialização atual. Prazo de 10 s. Retenção e permissões podem limitar a leitura; nenhum resultado não significa ausência de falhas. Mensagens podem conter nomes, caminhos e identificadores. A consulta não altera logs e não associa eventos a um benchmark específico. Os contextos CPU/memória, GPU/gráficos e PCIe são indicações por marcadores do texto, com o motivo exibido em cada registro. Contagens são de mensagens, não de falhas; uma mensagem pode ter vários contextos. Mensagens não reconhecidas permanecem visíveis." Classes="sub" />
                    </Expander>
                    <Border Height="1" Background="{DynamicResource Line}" Margin="0,6" />
                    <TextBlock Text="COMPARAÇÃO POR SESSÃO" Classes="label" />
                  </StackPanel>
                </Border>
              </ScrollViewer>
            </TabItem>
          </TabControl>
        </Grid>
        <ScrollViewer x:Name="BenchPage" IsVisible="False" Grid.Column="1" HorizontalScrollBarVisibility="Disabled">
          <StackPanel Margin="32,28" Spacing="20">
            <StackPanel Spacing="6">
              <TextBlock Text="RESET BENCH" Classes="h1" />
              <TextBlock Text="Compare o desempenho da CPU, da memória e do armazenamento e execute stress de GPU." Classes="sub" />
              <TextBlock x:Name="MachineText" Classes="sub" />
            </StackPanel>
            <Border Classes="card">
              <StackPanel Spacing="12">
                <TextBlock Text="INTERRUPÇÃO POR TEMPERATURA DA CPU" Classes="label" />
                <TextBlock Text="Defina a temperatura da CPU que solicita a interrupção dos benchmarks." Classes="sub" />
                <CheckBox x:Name="ThermalEnabled" Content="Monitorar durante os benchmarks" IsChecked="True" />
                <StackPanel Orientation="Horizontal" Spacing="12">
                  <TextBlock Text="Limite (°C)" VerticalAlignment="Center" />
                  <NumericUpDown x:Name="ThermalLimit" Minimum="60" Maximum="100" Value="90" Increment="1" Width="120" FormatString="0" />
                </StackPanel>
                <TextBlock x:Name="ThermalStateText" Text="Aguardando leitura da CPU…" Classes="sub" />
                <Button x:Name="SaveSettingsButton" Content="SALVAR CONFIGURAÇÃO" Classes="outline" Click="SavePreferences" />
                <TextBlock Text="Com o monitoramento ativo, o teste só inicia com uma leitura recente abaixo do limite." Classes="sub" />
                <Expander Header="Mais detalhes" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" IsExpanded="False">
                  <TextBlock Text="O app solicita cancelamento quando a CPU atinge o limite, perde o sensor ou fica 5 s sem atualização. A interrupção depende da resposta dos sensores e do teste, podendo não ser imediata." Classes="sub" />
                </Expander>
              </StackPanel>
            </Border>
            <Border Classes="card">
              <StackPanel Spacing="14">
                <TextBlock Text="CPU · PATH TRACING" Classes="label" />
                <TextBlock Text="Uma cena, todos os detalhes" Classes="big" />
                <TextBlock Text="Compare a CPU com uma ou todas as threads ao renderizar a mesma imagem. A duração depende da CPU." Classes="sub" />
                <Expander Header="Mais detalhes" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" IsExpanded="False">
                  <TextBlock Text="A cena tem 960 × 540 pixels e 1024 amostras por pixel. O teste termina ao completar a imagem." Classes="sub" />
                </Expander>
                <Image x:Name="RenderImage" Height="250" Stretch="Uniform" />
                <WrapPanel>
                  <Button x:Name="SingleButton" Content="1 THREAD" Classes="outline" Click="RunSingle" Margin="0,0,8,8" />
                  <Button x:Name="MultiButton" Content="TODAS AS THREADS" Classes="primary" Click="RunMulti" Margin="0,0,0,8" />
                </WrapPanel>
                <ContentControl x:Name="RenderRunHost">
                  <StackPanel x:Name="BenchmarkRunPanel" Spacing="12">
                <TextBlock x:Name="StatusText" Text="Pronto para iniciar." TextWrapping="Wrap" />
                <ProgressBar x:Name="RunProgress" Minimum="0" Maximum="100" />
                <Button x:Name="CancelButton" Content="CANCELAR" Classes="outline" IsEnabled="False" Click="CancelRun" />
                  </StackPanel>
                </ContentControl>
              </StackPanel>
            </Border>
            <Border Classes="card">
              <StackPanel Spacing="12">
                <TextBlock Text="CPU · SIMULAÇÃO DE JOGO" Classes="label" />
                <TextBlock Text="30 mil unidades em batalha" Classes="big" />
                <TextBlock Text="Avalie a CPU em uma simulação de física, colisões e inteligência artificial." Classes="sub" />
                <Expander Header="Mais detalhes" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" IsExpanded="False">
                  <TextBlock Text="A simulação usa 30 mil unidades, com 2 s de aquecimento seguidos de 10 s de medição." Classes="sub" />
                </Expander>
                <Button x:Name="SimButton" Content="INICIAR SIMULAÇÃO" Classes="outline" Click="RunSimulation" />
                <ContentControl x:Name="SimulationRunHost" />
              </StackPanel>
            </Border>
            <Border Classes="card">
              <StackPanel Spacing="12">
                <TextBlock Text="Stress de GPU" Classes="big" />
                <TextBlock Text="Escolha qual placa de vídeo receberá a carga. A detecção inclui GPUs integradas e dedicadas disponíveis no Vulkan." Classes="sub" />
                <TextBlock x:Name="GpuDetectionText" Text="Aguardando detecção automática…" Classes="sub" />
                <TextBlock Text="GPU a testar" />
                <ComboBox x:Name="GpuSelector" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" PlaceholderText="Selecione uma GPU" SelectionChanged="GpuSelectionChanged" AutomationProperties.Name="GPU a testar">
                  <ComboBox.ItemTemplate>
                    <DataTemplate><TextBlock Text="{Binding Label}" TextWrapping="Wrap" /></DataTemplate>
                  </ComboBox.ItemTemplate>
                </ComboBox>
                <TextBlock x:Name="GpuSelectionText" Text="Selecione uma GPU para verificar a disponibilidade do stress." Classes="sub" />
                <Button x:Name="GpuRefreshButton" Content="ATUALIZAR GPUs" Classes="outline" Click="RefreshGpuDevices" />
                <WrapPanel>
                  <StackPanel Spacing="4" Margin="0,0,16,8">
                    <TextBlock Text="Duração (min)" />
                    <NumericUpDown x:Name="GpuMinutes" Minimum="1" Maximum="5" Value="1" Increment="1" FormatString="0" Width="140" AutomationProperties.Name="Duração do stress GPU em minutos" />
                  </StackPanel>
                  <StackPanel Spacing="4" Margin="0,0,0,8">
                    <TextBlock Text="Limite GPU (°C)" />
                    <NumericUpDown x:Name="GpuLimit" Minimum="60" Maximum="90" Value="80" Increment="1" FormatString="0" Width="140" AutomationProperties.Name="Limite térmico da GPU escolhida" />
                  </StackPanel>
                </WrapPanel>
                <TextBlock Text="A carga aumenta consumo e temperatura. A proteção térmica da GPU é obrigatória. O teste roda sem janela de renderização; acompanhe o progresso aqui." Classes="sub" />
                <Button x:Name="GpuStartButton" Content="INICIAR STRESS DA GPU" Classes="primary" Click="RunGpuStress" IsEnabled="False" />
                <ContentControl x:Name="GpuRunHost" />
                <TextBlock x:Name="GpuResultText" Text="Nenhum stress de GPU executado. Resultados e interrupções ficam no histórico abaixo e nas exportações." Classes="sub" />
                <Expander Header="Mais detalhes" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" IsExpanded="False">
                  <TextBlock Text="Somente a GPU selecionada recebe a carga. NVIDIA usa NVML; Intel e AMD precisam expor sensores hwmon ligados ao mesmo dispositivo PCI. Sem temperatura própria válida, o teste não inicia. Cancelar ou fechar aguarda o frame em execução; falhas do driver podem atrasar o encerramento. A cena Vulkan é renderizada fora da tela, em 640×360, sem score comparável ao Windows ou certificação de estabilidade." Classes="sub" />
                </Expander>
              </StackPanel>
            </Border>
            <Border Classes="card">
              <StackPanel Spacing="12">
                <TextBlock Text="MEMÓRIA RAM" Classes="label" />
                <TextBlock Text="Meça o tempo de acesso à memória e a quantidade de dados transferida por segundo." Classes="sub" />
                <TextBlock Text="Latência e largura de banda" Classes="big" />
                <TextBlock Text="A latência usa cerca de 272 MiB; o teste de banda usa cerca de 2 GiB de RAM. Execute um teste por vez, com memória disponível." Classes="sub" />
                <WrapPanel>
                  <Button x:Name="LatencyButton" Content="LATÊNCIA" Classes="outline" Click="RunLatency" Margin="0,0,8,8" />
                  <Button x:Name="BandwidthButton" Content="BANDA" Classes="outline" Click="RunBandwidth" Margin="0,0,0,8" />
                </WrapPanel>
                <ContentControl x:Name="MemoryRunHost" />
                <Button x:Name="ReleaseMemoryButton" Content="LIBERAR MEMÓRIA DOS TESTES" Classes="outline" Click="ReleaseTestMemory" />
                <TextBlock Text="Libera memória temporária do app sem apagar seus resultados. A recuperação pode ocorrer em etapas: aguarde a conclusão e a atualização do monitor do sistema antes de repetir." Classes="sub" />
                <Expander Header="Mais detalhes" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch">
                  <TextBlock Text="Após sucesso, erro ou cancelamento, uma nova limpeza pode recuperar objetos que ficaram disponíveis depois da coleta anterior. Isso não significa que vários cliques sejam sempre necessários. A ação pode causar breve pausa; o uso total de RAM pode não voltar ao valor anterior e uma nova coleta pode não reduzir o número exibido." Classes="sub" />
                </Expander>
                <TextBlock x:Name="MemoryCleanupStatusText" Classes="sub" />
              </StackPanel>
            </Border>
            <Border Classes="card">
              <StackPanel Spacing="12">
                <TextBlock Text="DISCO E CARREGAMENTO" Classes="label" />
                <TextBlock Text="Pasta do volume a testar" Classes="big" />
                <TextBox x:Name="DiskBenchmarkPath" Watermark="Caminho absoluto de uma pasta existente, por exemplo /home/usuario" />
                <TextBlock Text="Meça leitura, escrita e carregamento na pasta escolhida. Reserve 1,1 GiB livres; os testes gravam arquivos temporários." Classes="sub" />
                <Expander Header="Mais detalhes" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" IsExpanded="False">
                  <TextBlock Text="Disco: arquivo de 512 MiB, operações sequenciais e testes aleatórios de 4 s cada. A escrita aleatória adiciona volume variável de gravação. Carregamento: pacote de 512 MiB e 1000 arquivos de 64 KiB. Os arquivos exclusivos são removidos ao terminar ou cancelar." Classes="sub" />
                </Expander>
                <TextBlock Text="O limite de temperatura monitora a CPU, não o disco." Classes="sub" />
                <Expander Header="Mais detalhes" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" IsExpanded="False">
                  <TextBlock Text="Linux x64 com I/O direto confirmado pelo sistema de arquivos. Se indisponível, o teste informa erro. No Btrfs, apenas os arquivos temporários usam NoCoW/sem compressão; não representa arquivos Btrfs comuns. Não acessa discos brutos. Cache do dispositivo e metadados ainda podem influenciar. O limite térmico acima monitora a CPU, não o disco." Classes="sub" />
                </Expander>
                <WrapPanel>
                  <Button x:Name="DiskBenchmarkButton" Content="TESTAR DISCO" Classes="outline" Click="RunDiskBenchmark" Margin="0,0,8,8" />
                  <Button x:Name="LoadingBenchmarkButton" Content="TESTAR CARREGAMENTO" Classes="outline" Click="RunLoadingBenchmark" Margin="0,0,0,8" />
                </WrapPanel>
                <ContentControl x:Name="DiskRunHost" />
              </StackPanel>
            </Border>
            <Border Classes="card">
              <StackPanel Spacing="12">
                <TextBlock Text="REGISTRO DESTA SESSÃO" Classes="label" />
                <TextBlock Text="Acompanhe as mensagens dos testes executados desde que o app foi aberto." Classes="sub" />
                <Button x:Name="ClearSessionButton" Content="LIMPAR REGISTRO DA SESSÃO" Classes="outline" Click="ClearSessionHistory" />
                <TextBlock Text="Limpa somente o texto desta sessão. O histórico salvo e as exportações não são alterados." Classes="sub" />
                <TextBox x:Name="ResultsText" IsReadOnly="True" AcceptsReturn="True" TextWrapping="Wrap" MinHeight="90" />
              </StackPanel>
            </Border>
            <Border Classes="card">
              <StackPanel Spacing="12">
                <TextBlock Text="HISTÓRICO · ÚLTIMAS 200 EXECUÇÕES" Classes="label" />
                <TextBlock Text="Consulte e exporte os resultados e interrupções das últimas 200 execuções." Classes="sub" />
                <Expander Header="Mais detalhes" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" IsExpanded="False">
                  <TextBlock Text="A configuração é salva pelo botão SALVAR CONFIGURAÇÃO ou ao iniciar um teste. Resultados e interrupções são gravados ao terminar. Se houver falha de gravação, use SALVAR PENDÊNCIAS / ATUALIZAR." Classes="sub" />
                </Expander>
                <TextBlock x:Name="PersistenceStatusText" Classes="sub" />
                <Button x:Name="RetrySaveButton" Content="SALVAR PENDÊNCIAS / ATUALIZAR" Classes="outline" Click="RetryPersistence" />
                <WrapPanel>
                  <Button x:Name="ExportHtmlButton" Content="EXPORTAR HTML" Classes="outline" Click="ExportHtml" Margin="0,0,8,8" />
                  <Button x:Name="ExportJsonButton" Content="EXPORTAR JSON" Classes="outline" Click="ExportJson" Margin="0,0,0,8" />
                </WrapPanel>
                <TextBlock Text="Exporta o histórico disponível, incluindo contexto do sistema e registros pendentes. Escolha onde salvar o arquivo." Classes="sub" />
                <TextBlock x:Name="ExportStatusText" Classes="sub" />
                <Button x:Name="ClearHistoryButton" Content="LIMPAR HISTÓRICO SALVO" Classes="outline" Click="RequestHistoryClear" />
                <TextBlock Text="Remove os registros salvos e pendentes usados nas próximas exportações. Arquivos já exportados e configurações são preservados." Classes="sub" />
                <StackPanel x:Name="ClearHistoryConfirmation" IsVisible="False" Spacing="8">
                  <TextBlock Text="Apagar o histórico salvo e os registros pendentes? Exporte antes se quiser guardar uma cópia." TextWrapping="Wrap" />
                  <WrapPanel>
                    <Button x:Name="ConfirmClearHistoryButton" Content="APAGAR HISTÓRICO" Classes="outline" Click="ConfirmHistoryClear" Margin="0,0,8,8" />
                    <Button Content="VOLTAR" Classes="outline" Click="DismissHistoryClear" Margin="0,0,0,8" />
                  </WrapPanel>
                </StackPanel>
                <TextBlock x:Name="HistoryClearStatusText" Classes="sub" />
                <TextBox x:Name="HistoryText" IsReadOnly="True" AcceptsReturn="True" TextWrapping="Wrap" MinHeight="140" MaxHeight="360" />
              </StackPanel>
            </Border>
          </StackPanel>
        </ScrollViewer>
      </Grid>
    </Window>

    using System.Globalization;
    
    namespace ResetExplode;
    
    public static class BenchmarkMethodology
    {
        public static Dictionary<string, string> Parameters(string test)
        {
            var result = new Dictionary<string, string>
            {
                ["methodology"] = test == "ram-bandwidth" ? "linux-ram-bandwidth-v2" : $"linux-{test}-v1",
                ["logicalProcessors"] = Environment.ProcessorCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
                ["telemetry"] = "active; may affect timing",
                ["crossPlatformComparison"] = "not validated"
            };
            switch (test)
            {
                case "gpu-stress":
                    result["measurement"] = "Vulkan 1.1 offscreen torus; completed frames; no score or stability verdict";
                    result["resolution"] = "640x360";
                    result["shells"] = "32";
                    result["aluIterations"] = "32";
                    result["duration"] = "render loop only; excludes initialization/readback; waits for submitted frame";
                    result["thermal"] = "mandatory selected PCI GPU sensor; NVIDIA NVML or device-local hwmon; no CPU fallback";
                    break;
                case "cpu-ram-stress":
                    result["measurement"] = "bounded CPU FMA/sqrt/sin/integer load and RAM pattern write/verify; no performance score or stability verdict";
                    result["memory"] = "explicit MiB, maximum 1024; at most one quarter of observed MemAvailable/runtime budget; availability may change";
                    result["duration"] = "includes allocation; incomplete RAM passes not counted; cooperative deadline";
                    result["thermal"] = "mandatory CPU temperature watchdog; does not monitor DIMM temperature";
                    result["hardwareErrors"] = "EDAC and journal are separate manual observations, not automatically attributed to this workload";
                    break;
                case "disk-direct":
                case "disk-loading":
                    result["io"] = "O_DIRECT; alignment via STATX_DIOALIGN or Btrfs sector ioctl; 4096-byte aligned buffers/offsets; writes O_DSYNC";
                    result["btrfs"] = "temporary files only: verified NoCoW/NoCompress; not representative of default CoW/checksummed files";
                    result["fileBytes"] = "536870912";
                    result["sequentialBlockBytes"] = "1048576";
                    result["units"] = "MiB/s (1048576 bytes); IOPS; mean operation duration in microseconds";
                    result["cacheLimits"] = "device/controller and metadata caches may contribute; no global cache changes";
                    result["data"] = "seeded 1 MiB buffer reused; compression/deduplication may affect results";
                    result["repetitions"] = "one run; no aggregation or cross-platform score";
                    result["parallelism"] = "up to 8 synchronous workers, not guaranteed device queue depth";
                    if (test == "disk-direct")
                    {
                        result["randomBlockBytes"] = "4096";
                        result["randomPhaseSeconds"] = "4";
                        result["measurement"] = "sequential write then read; random read/write single worker; random read up to 8 workers; allocation included in sequential write";
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        result["assetCount"] = "1000";
                        result["assetBytes"] = "65536";
                        result["warmup"] = "one unmeasured asset read pass; preparation writes excluded";
                        result["measurement"] = "pack read plus measured assets open/read/close; includes statx and worker scheduling; not real game load time";
                    }
                    break;
                case "cpu-render":
                    result["warmup"] = "none";
                    result["measurement"] = "one complete pass; samples/seconds/10000 points";
                    break;
                case "cpu-simulation":
                    result["warmupSeconds"] = "2";
                    result["low1Definition"] = "1000 / sorted frame time at floor(count * 0.99), clamped to last index";
                    result["measurement"] = "synthetic CPU simulation; not game rendering FPS";
                    break;
                case "ram-latency":
                    result["bufferBytes"] = "268435456";
                    result["orderBytes"] = "16777216";
                    result["seed"] = "42";
                    result["strideBytes"] = "64";
                    result["warmupSteps"] = "200000";
                    result["measuredSteps"] = "20000000";
                    result["measurement"] = "dependent reads; allocation and permutation excluded; cache/TLB may contribute";
                    break;
                case "ram-bandwidth":
                    result["bufferBytesEach"] = "1073741824";
                    result["buffers"] = "2";
                    result["repetitions"] = "3";
                    result["aggregation"] = "independent maximum for read and copy";
                    result["warmup"] = "source initialized outside timing; no separate warmup; destination first touch may be timed";
                    result["units"] = "decimal GB/s; read=bytes/seconds; copy=2*bytes/seconds";
                    result["measurement"] = "all elements; 65536-element cancellation chunks; logical traffic, not physical DRAM bandwidth";
                    break;
            }
            return result;
        }
    }
    <Window xmlns="https://github.com/avaloniaui"
            xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml"
            xmlns:hw="clr-namespace:ResetExplode.Hardware;assembly=ResetExplode.Hardware"
            x:Class="ResetExplode.MainWindow" Title="RESET EXPLODE"
            Width="1180" Height="850" MinWidth="820" MinHeight="620">
      <Grid ColumnDefinitions="210,*">
        <Border Background="{DynamicResource Panel}" BorderBrush="{DynamicResource Line}" BorderThickness="0,0,1,0" Padding="20,28">
          <Grid RowDefinitions="Auto,Auto,Auto,* ,Auto">
            <Image Source="avares://ResetExplode/Assets/logo.png" Height="52" Stretch="Uniform" />
            <TextBlock Grid.Row="1" Text="RESET EXPLODE" Classes="label" Margin="0,40,0,12" />
            <StackPanel Grid.Row="2" Spacing="8">
              <Button x:Name="SystemNav" Content="Sistema e sensores" Classes="primary" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" Click="ShowSystem" Padding="12,11" />
              <Button x:Name="BenchNav" Content="Benchmark" Classes="outline" HorizontalAlignment="Stretch" Click="ShowBench" Padding="12,11" />
            </StackPanel>
            <StackPanel Grid.Row="4" Spacing="8">
              <TextBlock Text="LINUX · VERSÃO INICIAL" Classes="label" />
              <TextBlock x:Name="SidebarCpuText" Text="CPU · aguardando leitura" Classes="sub" />
              <TextBlock x:Name="SidebarRamText" Text="RAM · aguardando leitura" Classes="sub" />
            </StackPanel>
          </Grid>
        </Border>
        <Grid x:Name="SystemPage" Grid.Column="1" RowDefinitions="Auto,*" Margin="24,24">
          <StackPanel Grid.Row="0" Spacing="6" Margin="0,0,0,18">
              <TextBlock Text="Sistema e sensores" Classes="h1" />
              <TextBlock Text="Escolha uma seção para consultar informações e acompanhar leituras em tempo real." Classes="sub" />
              <TextBlock x:Name="TelemetryStatusText" Text="Detectando hardware…" Classes="sub" />
            </StackPanel>
          <TabControl x:Name="SystemSections" Grid.Row="1" Classes="system-tabs"
                      SelectedIndex="0" HorizontalContentAlignment="Stretch" VerticalContentAlignment="Stretch">
            <TabControl.ItemsPanel>
              <ItemsPanelTemplate>
                <WrapPanel Orientation="Horizontal" />
              </ItemsPanelTemplate>

    using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
    
    namespace ResetExplode;
    
    /// <summary>Somente arquivos próprios; nunca remove recursivamente a pasta selecionada.</summary>
    internal sealed class BenchmarkWorkspace : IDisposable
    {
        public string DirectoryPath { get; }
        private readonly HashSet<string> _created = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        public BenchmarkWorkspace(string parent, long requiredBytes)
        {
            LinuxDirectFile.RequirePlatform();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(parent) || parent.Contains('\0') || !Path.IsPathFullyQualified(parent) || !Directory.Exists(parent))
                throw new ArgumentException("Escolha uma pasta existente com caminho absoluto.");
            if (Statvfs(parent, out var space) != 0) throw LinuxDirectFile.Error("Espaço livre indisponível", parent);
            if ((decimal)space.Available * space.FragmentSize < requiredBytes)
                throw new IOException($"Espaço insuficiente: necessários {requiredBytes / 1048576} MiB livres, incluindo margem.");
            DirectoryPath = Path.Combine(parent, ".reset-explode-bench-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
            if (Mkdir(DirectoryPath, 0x1C0) != 0) throw LinuxDirectFile.Error("Falha ao criar pasta exclusiva", DirectoryPath); // 0700
        }
        public string FilePath(string name)
        {
            if (Path.GetFileName(name) != name || name is "." or ".." || string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) throw new ArgumentException("Nome inválido.");
            string path = Path.Combine(DirectoryPath, name);
            _created.Add(path);
            return path;
        }
        public void Dispose()
        {
            var failures = new List<Exception>();
            foreach (string file in _created)
                try { File.Delete(file); } catch (Exception ex) { failures.Add(ex); }
            try { Directory.Delete(DirectoryPath); } catch (Exception ex) { failures.Add(ex); }
            if (failures.Count != 0)
                throw new IOException($"Não foi possível limpar todos os arquivos temporários em {DirectoryPath}. Verifique essa pasta; resultado não publicado.", new AggregateException(failures));
        }
        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Explicit, Size = 112)]
        private struct SpaceData
        {
            [FieldOffset(8)] public ulong FragmentSize;
            [FieldOffset(32)] public ulong Available;
        }
        [DllImport("libc", EntryPoint = "statvfs", SetLastError = true)]
        private static extern int Statvfs([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPUTF8Str)] string path, out SpaceData data);
        [DllImport("libc", EntryPoint = "mkdir", SetLastError = true)]
        private static extern int Mkdir([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPUTF8Str)] string path, uint mode);
    }
        }
    
        private void UpdateThermalStatus()
        {
            if (ThermalEnabled.IsChecked != true)
            {
                ThermalStateText.Text = "Desativado: os testes não serão interrompidos por temperatura ou perda de leitura.";
                return;
            }
            double limit = (double)(ThermalLimit.Value ?? (decimal)CpuThermalPolicy.DefaultLimit);
            var policy = new CpuThermalPolicy(limit);
            var reading = Volatile.Read(ref _thermalReading);
            var stop = policy.Evaluate(reading);
            ThermalStateText.Text = stop != null ? stop.Message
                : $"CPU: {reading!.Celsius:N1} °C · limite: {limit:N0} °C · leitura recente disponível.";
        }
    
        private void CancelRun(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => RequestCancellation();
    
        private void RequestCancellation()
        {
            _cancellation?.Cancel();
            CancelButton.IsEnabled = false;
            StatusText.Text = "Cancelando; aguardando o trabalho em andamento terminar…";
        }
    
        private void RefreshProgress()
        {
            if (_cancellation?.IsCancellationRequested == true) return;
            StatusText.Text = $"{_currentTest} · {_elapsed.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:N0} s";
            var disk = Volatile.Read(ref _diskProgress);
            var stress = Volatile.Read(ref _stressProgress);
            var gpu = Volatile.Read(ref _gpuProgress);
            if (gpu != null)
            {
                StatusText.Text += $" · {gpu.Frames} frames · GPU {gpu.Temperature:N0} °C";
                RunProgress.IsIndeterminate = false;
                RunProgress.Value = Math.Min(100, 100 * gpu.Seconds / _gpuDurationSeconds);
            }
            else if (stress != null)
            {
                StatusText.Text += $" · {stress.AllocatedBytes / StressTest.MiB} MiB RAM · {stress.MemoryPasses} passada(s)";
                RunProgress.IsIndeterminate = false;
                RunProgress.Value = Math.Min(100, 100 * stress.Seconds / _stressDurationSeconds);
            }
            else if (disk != null)
            {
                StatusText.Text += $" · {disk.Phase}";
                RunProgress.IsIndeterminate = false;
                RunProgress.Value = 100 * disk.Fraction;
            }
            else if (_renderer != null)
            {
                RefreshBitmap();
                RunProgress.Value = 100.0 * _renderer.BucketsDone / Math.Max(1, _renderer.BucketsTotal);
            }
            else if (_simulation != null)
            {
                RunProgress.Value = 100 * _simulation.Progress;
                StatusText.Text += _elapsed.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < 2 ? " · aquecimento" : $" · {_simulation.CurrentFps:N1} FPS";
            }
        }
    
        private void RefreshBitmap()
        {
            if (_renderer == null) return;
    # Craft floor
    
    Load this after the direction is settled, and build without announcing the checklist. A pinned brief or the committed visual world overrides anything here except a line marked as a ban; your own habit does not. When the design hook is active it already enforces the mechanical checks below as you edit: act on its findings instead of re-auditing each rule.
    
    ## Verify
    
    Each of these is a check on the built result, not an intention. Run them together in each batched look at the render, not as separate screenshot trips; the checks share one render.
    
    - **Contrast:** body and placeholder text ≥4.5:1, large text ≥3:1. On colored surfaces tint secondary text from that hue or the foreground; never gray.
    - **Depth:** shadows carry an offset and a soft blur. A zero-offset colored halo is decoration.
    - **Spacing:** tight groups, generous separation, more space above a heading than below it. Read the computed values.
    - **Type:** body measure 65–75ch, display max 6rem, tracking floor -0.04em, balanced headings, obvious scale and weight steps. Run the real copy at every breakpoint and fix what overflows.
    - **Motion:** one authored moment, not scattered effects and not one identical entrance on every section. Exponential ease-out from an already-visible default. Reach past transform and opacity: blur, backdrop-filter, clip-path, mask, and shadow belong to the palette when they stay smooth.
    - **States:** hover, disabled, loading, error, empty. Plus real content, working controls, responsive composition, keyboard focus.
    - **Browser surfaces:** the parts you did not draw still carry the design. Text selection, the caret, custom scrollbars, focus rings, underline offset, and the numerals in tabular data all ship with browser defaults that belong to no design system. Theme them from the palette. This is the cheapest signal that a page was built rather than assembled, and the one models skip most reliably.
    - **Copy:** the product's own language. Controls name their action; errors name the problem and the recovery.
    - **Coverage:** every brief requirement present and findable within seconds.
    
    ## Refuse
    
    These are the category's defaults, not bans: the brief's own words can earn any of them. Reaching for one when the axis is free means you were not deciding; recognizing that means rewriting the element, not softening it.
    
    Page scaffolds:
    
    - Same-size cards of icon plus heading plus text as the page structure. Cards are the lazy container; nested cards are always wrong.
    - The hero-metric template: big number, small label, supporting stats, accent.
    - A kicker or eyebrow above a heading. This one is a ban, not a default: no brief earns it back. The heading carries its own weight; delete the label and let the heading speak.
    - Section numbers (01 / 02 / 03) unless the sequence itself carries information the reader needs.
    - A modal for a task that needs neither interruption nor protected focus.
    
    Surface habits:
    
    - Gradient text. Emphasis comes from weight or size.
    - Glass and blur as decoration rather than as a specific effect.
    - A colored `border-left` or `border-right` above 1px on cards, list items, callouts, or alerts.
    - Hard offset shadows (`box-shadow: 4px 4px 0`) outside a world that is actually neobrutalist. The zero-blur block shadow is a costume, not a depth system; a world that did not choose it never earns it as a default.
    - Sparklines, progress rings, and soft-shadowed rounded rectangles standing in for content.
    - Monospace as a costume for "technical" rather than for code, data, or measurement.
    - A system display face (Impact, Arial Black, the platform sans) as the display voice of an own-world page. Source and self-host a face whose character matches the approved lettering; the closest installed font is a failure, not a fallback.
    - Unicode glyphs or emoji standing in for an icon system. Icons are drawn, from a real library or authored SVG, in one consistent stroke and weight.
    - Geometric masks standing in for organic contours. A circle, polygon, or radial-gradient cutout approximating a photographic subject's edge is the cheap version of the effect and reads worse than omitting it. Derive an alpha matte from the actual image, or produce a cut-out asset.
    - Light or dark picked by category. Pick it from the use scene: who, where, under what ambient light.
    
    - Tracking stops at -0.04em. -0.02 to -0.03em usually reads better.
    - Declare elevation once, border or shadow. A 1px border under a wide soft shadow is the ghost card. Card radii stay at 12–16px; pills are for small controls.
    - Real illustration or none. Sketch-style SVG scenes, `loose-sketch` / `doodle` class names, and `feTurbulence` grain read as amateur. This bans SVG imitating pictures, never SVG doing geometry: crisp vector shapes, diagrams, animated linework, and shader-driven effects remain first-class media. A shaded, perspectived, or figure-bearing illustration is a picture even in line-art style; geometry means shapes a session can specify exactly.
    - Backgrounds are surfaces, textured only from the subject's world. `repeating-linear-gradient` stripes and two-axis grid overlays need an actual canvas, map, blueprint, or measuring tool under them.
    - Claims and configuration come from supplied truth; label illustrative values honestly. Naming a concept and then ironizing it is not a claim.
    
    The floor holds the mechanics; it never picks the direction. With every check green, spend the page on the committed world, and when torn between refined and committed, commit.

    namespace ResetExplode;
    
    public sealed record LinuxSettings
    {
        public int SchemaVersion { get; init; } = 1;
        public bool ThermalEnabled { get; init; } = true;
        public double ThermalLimit { get; init; } = CpuThermalPolicy.DefaultLimit;
    }
    
    public enum BenchmarkOutcome { Completed, NotStarted, ThermalStopped, Cancelled, Failed }
    public sealed record BenchmarkMeasurement(string Summary, double? Score, Dictionary<string, double> Metrics);
    
    public sealed record BenchmarkHistoryEntry
    {
        public Guid Id { get; init; }
        public DateTimeOffset Date { get; init; }
        public string Test { get; init; } = "";
        public string Name { get; init; } = "";
        public BenchmarkOutcome Outcome { get; init; }
        public string Summary { get; init; } = "";
        public double? Score { get; init; }
        public Dictionary<string, double> Metrics { get; init; } = new();
        public Dictionary<string, string> Context { get; init; } = new();
        public bool ThermalEnabled { get; init; }
        public double ThermalLimit { get; init; } = CpuThermalPolicy.DefaultLimit;
    }
    
    public sealed record BenchmarkHistory
    {
        public int SchemaVersion { get; init; } = 1;
        public List<BenchmarkHistoryEntry> Entries { get; init; } = new();
    }
    
    public sealed record StoredData<T>(T Value, string? Notice = null, bool CanWrite = true, bool NeedsBackup = false);
    
    public sealed record XdgPaths(string SettingsFile, string HistoryFile)
    {
        public static XdgPaths ForCurrentUser() => Resolve(
            Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.UserProfile),
            Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("XDG_CONFIG_HOME"), Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("XDG_STATE_HOME"));
    
        public static XdgPaths Resolve(string home, string? config, string? state)
        {
            if (!Path.IsPathFullyQualified(home)) throw new ArgumentException("Diretório do usuário não disponível.", nameof(home));
            string Base(string? candidate, string fallback) => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(candidate) && Path.IsPathFullyQualified(candidate)
                ? candidate : Path.Combine(home, fallback);
            return new(Path.Combine(Base(config, ".config"), "ResetExplode", "settings.json"),
                Path.Combine(Base(state, ".local/state"), "ResetExplode", "benchmarks.json"));
        }
    }
        }
    
        private void RenderHistory()
        {
            var pendingIds = _pendingHistory.Select(e => e.Id).ToHashSet();
            var rows = _pendingHistory.Concat(_history).DistinctBy(e => e.Id)
                .OrderByDescending(e => e.Date).Take(LocalPersistence.MaximumEntries);
            HistoryText.Text = string.Join(Environment.NewLine + Environment.NewLine, rows.Select(e =>
            {
                string state = e.Outcome switch
                {
                    BenchmarkOutcome.Completed => "CONCLUÍDO",
                    BenchmarkOutcome.NotStarted => "NÃO INICIADO",
                    BenchmarkOutcome.ThermalStopped => "INTERROMPIDO",
                    BenchmarkOutcome.Cancelled => "CANCELADO",
                    _ => "ERRO"
                };
                string pending = pendingIds.Contains(e.Id) ? " · NÃO SALVO" : "";
                return $"{e.Date.ToLocalTime():dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss} · {e.Name} · {state}{pending}\n{e.Summary}";
            }));
        }
    
        private void UpdatePersistenceStatus()
        {
            string notices = string.Join(Environment.NewLine, new[] { _settingsNotice, _historyNotice }
                .Where(s => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)));
            PersistenceStatusText.Text = notices.Length > 0 ? notices
                : $"Armazenamento local pronto · {_history.Count} registro(s) · {_pendingHistory.Count} pendente(s).";
        }
    }

        cat > Linux/Core/Benchmarks/BenchmarkCategory.cs <<'EOF'
        namespace ResetExplode;
        
        public static class BenchmarkCategory
        {
            // Legacy combined stress remains visible in both component histories.
            public static bool Includes(string test, string component) => component switch
            {
                "cpu" => test is "cpu-render" or "cpu-simulation" or "cpu-stress" or "cpu-ram-stress",
                "ram" => test is "ram-latency" or "ram-bandwidth" or "ram-stress" or "cpu-ram-stress",
                "gpu" => test == "gpu-stress",
                "disk" => test is "disk-direct" or "disk-loading",
                _ => false
            };
        }
        EOF
        cat > Linux/SmokeTests/Core/BenchmarkCategoryChecks.cs <<'EOF'
        using ResetExplode;
        
        internal static class BenchmarkCategoryChecks
        {
            public static void Run()
            {
                foreach (var (test, component) in new[] {
                    ("cpu-render", "cpu"), ("cpu-simulation", "cpu"), ("cpu-stress", "cpu"),
                    ("ram-latency", "ram"), ("ram-bandwidth", "ram"), ("ram-stress", "ram"),
                    ("gpu-stress", "gpu"), ("disk-direct", "disk"), ("disk-loading", "disk") })
                    foreach (var category in new[] { "cpu", "ram", "gpu", "disk" })
                        if (BenchmarkCategory.Includes(test, category) != (component == category))
                            throw new Exception($"Histórico incorreto: {test} em {category}");
                if (!BenchmarkCategory.Includes("cpu-ram-stress", "cpu") || !BenchmarkCategory.Includes("cpu-ram-stress", "ram") ||
                    BenchmarkCategory.Includes("unknown", "cpu")) throw new Exception("Histórico legado/ desconhecido incorreto.");
                foreach (var test in new[] { "cpu-stress", "ram-stress" })
                    if (!BenchmarkMethodology.Parameters(test)["thermal"].Contains("mandatory"))
                        throw new Exception("Metodologia de stress sem proteção obrigatória.");
                Console.WriteLine("OK: históricos por componente, stress legado e metodologia dos stresses separados.");
            }
        }
        EOF

    docs/VALIDACAO_ESTABILIDADE_MEMORIA.md:16:- [ ] Rodar CPU, RAM e combinação por 1 minuto; conferir progresso, passadas, resultado sem pontuação e histórico/exportações na página Benchmark.
    docs/VALIDACAO_ESTABILIDADE_MEMORIA.md:24:RAM usa 16–1024 MiB explicitamente; padrão 256 MiB. Prazo de 1–30 minutos inclui alocação. Margem de memória é uma estimativa momentânea, não uma reserva ou proteção contra OOM externo. Proteção térmica observa CPU, não DIMMs. Testes curtos sem passada RAM completa são apresentados como inconclusivos.
    docs/VALIDACAO_ESTABILIDADE_MEMORIA.md:40:## Estabilidade — EDAC
    docs/VALIDACAO_ESTABILIDADE_MEMORIA.md:42:Nova sub-aba **Sistema e sensores → Estabilidade**, com consulta manual dos controladores `/sys/devices/system/edac/mc/mc*`. Exibe nome, erros corrigidos (`ce_count`), não corrigidos (`ue_count`) e segundos desde reset (`seconds_since_reset`). Usa inteiros de 64 bits sem sinal e mantém ausência/dado inválido como indisponível. Não soma novamente DIMMs/canais aos totais do controlador e não reseta contadores.
    docs/VALIDACAO_ESTABILIDADE_MEMORIA.md:56:- [X] Estabilidade: antes da consulta, mostrar que ainda não foi realizada. Atualizar e conferir dados disponíveis ou mensagem explícita de indisponibilidade.
    docs/VALIDACAO_ESTABILIDADE_MEMORIA.md:64:- Progresso, estado e CANCELAR acompanham o cartão da ação executada (CPU, simulação, RAM ou disco/carregamento). Registro da sessão passa a ter cartão próprio.
    docs/VALIDACAO_ESTABILIDADE_MEMORIA.md:99:Implementada consulta manual em Sistema e sensores → Estabilidade → CONSULTAR LOGS. Usa `journalctl --kernel --boot=0 --priority=0..4 --lines=500 --reverse --output=json --no-pager`, sem shell nem elevação. Exibe até 500 registros mais recentes de emergência a aviso da inicialização atual, com horário local, prioridade numérica e mensagem. Não classifica GPU/PCIe/CPU nem conta falhas físicas: várias mensagens podem descrever um único evento ou uma condição sem falha de hardware. A coleta não é associada ao intervalo de um benchmark.
    docs/VALIDACAO_ESTABILIDADE_MEMORIA.md:142:Em Estabilidade, CRIAR NOVA REFERÊNCIA captura EDAC, identificação da inicialização e limites de tempo monotônico. COMPARAR COM REFERÊNCIA faz nova leitura e consulta os logs. Comparações repetidas usam a mesma referência; criar outra substitui a anterior, e fechar o app a descarta. Não inicia benchmarks, zera contadores ou persiste a referência. Operação cancelável; fechamento cancela e aguarda. Consulta EDAC/logs avulsa continua independente.

    using Avalonia.Interactivity;
    using ResetExplode.Hardware;
    
    namespace ResetExplode;
    
    public partial class MainWindow
    {
        private StressProgress? _stressProgress;
        private double _stressDurationSeconds;
    
        private async void RunCpuStress(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => await RunStressAsync(true);
    
        private async void RunRamStress(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => await RunStressAsync(false);
    
        private async Task RunStressAsync(bool cpu)
        {
            if (_cancellation != null || _closeWhenStopped || _exporting || _cleaningMemory || _clearingHistory ||
                _latencyCancellation != null || _throughputCancellation != null || _dnsCancellation != null) return;
            bool ram = !cpu;
            long bytes = ram ? (long)(StressMemory.Value ?? 256) * StressTest.MiB : 0;
            var duration = TimeSpan.FromMinutes((double)((cpu ? CpuStressMinutes : RamStressMinutes).Value ?? 5));
            _stressDurationSeconds = duration.TotalSeconds;
            (cpu ? CpuStressResultText : RamStressResultText).Text = "Aguardando pré-checagem térmica e de memória…";
            try
            {
                await RunAsync(cpu ? "cpu-stress" : "ram-stress", cpu ? "Stress de CPU" : "Stress de RAM", ct =>
                {
                    if (ram)
                    {
                        var available = LinuxSystemInfoCollector.ReadMemory(new LinuxFiles()).AvailableBytes;
                        long runtimeBudget = GC.GetGCMemoryInfo().TotalAvailableMemoryBytes - GC.GetTotalMemory(false);
                        StressTest.ValidateMemory(bytes, available, runtimeBudget);
                    }
                    var result = StressTest.Run(cpu, bytes, duration, p => Volatile.Write(ref _stressProgress, p), ct);
                    string memory = !ram ? "RAM não selecionada" : result.MemoryPasses == 0
                        ? "RAM inconclusiva: nenhuma passada completa" : $"RAM: {result.MemoryPasses} passada(s) sem divergência observada";
                    return new BenchmarkMeasurement($"Carga encerrada após {result.Seconds:N1} s · {result.CpuThreads} thread(s) CPU · {memory}. Não comprova estabilidade.",
                        null, new() { ["seconds"] = result.Seconds, ["cpuThreads"] = result.CpuThreads,
                            ["allocatedBytes"] = result.AllocatedBytes, ["memoryPasses"] = result.MemoryPasses });
                }, new() { ["stressCpu"] = cpu.ToString(), ["memoryBytes"] = bytes.ToString(),
                    ["durationSeconds"] = duration.TotalSeconds.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) },
                    mandatoryThermalLimit: (double)((cpu ? CpuStressLimit : RamStressLimit).Value ?? 90));
            }
            finally
            {
                Volatile.Write(ref _stressProgress, null);
            }
        }
    }

        ## Benchmark por componente — 23/11/2026

    using Avalonia.Controls;
    using Avalonia.Interactivity;
    using ResetExplode.Hardware;
    
    namespace ResetExplode;
    
    public partial class MainWindow
    {
        private Task _gpuDiscoveryWork = Task.CompletedTask;
        private bool _gpuDiscovering;
        private GpuRenderProgress? _gpuProgress;
        private double _gpuDurationSeconds;
    
        private async void RefreshGpuDevices(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => await StartGpuDiscovery();
    
        private Task StartGpuDiscovery()
        {
            if (_gpuDiscovering || _closeWhenStopped || _cancellation != null) return _gpuDiscoveryWork;
            _gpuDiscoveryWork = DiscoverGpuDevicesAsync();
            return _gpuDiscoveryWork;
        }
    
        private async Task DiscoverGpuDevicesAsync()
        {
            string? selectedPci = (GpuSelector.SelectedItem as GpuStressChoice)?.Device.PciAddress;
            _gpuDiscovering = true;
            GpuSelector.ItemsSource = null;
            GpuDetectionText.Text = "Detectando GPUs e verificando sensores…";
            GpuSelectionText.Text = "A detecção não inicia carga gráfica.";
            UpdateGpuControls();
            try
            {
                var choices = await Task.Run(GpuStressDiscovery.Collect);
                if (_closeWhenStopped) return;
                GpuSelector.ItemsSource = choices;
                GpuSelector.SelectedItem = GpuStressDiscovery.RestoreSelection(choices, selectedPci);
                GpuDetectionText.Text = choices.Count == 0 ? "Nenhuma GPU Vulkan encontrada. Verifique o driver e atualize a lista."
                    : $"{choices.Count} dispositivo(s) detectado(s). Escolha a GPU que deseja testar.";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                if (!_closeWhenStopped) GpuDetectionText.Text = $"Detecção indisponível: {ex.Message} Verifique o driver Vulkan e tente atualizar.";
            }
            finally
            {
                _gpuDiscovering = false;
                UpdateGpuSelection();
            }
        }
    
        private void GpuSelectionChanged(object? sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e) => UpdateGpuSelection();
        private void UpdateGpuSelection()
        {
            if (GpuSelectionText == null) return; // Eventos durante InitializeComponent.
            GpuSelectionText.Text = (GpuSelector.SelectedItem as GpuStressChoice)?.Status ?? "Selecione uma GPU. Nenhum dispositivo é escolhido automaticamente.";
            UpdateGpuControls();
        }
    
        private void UpdateGpuControls()
        {
            if (GpuStartButton == null) return;
            bool available = _cancellation == null && !_gpuDiscovering && !_closeWhenStopped && !_exporting &&
                !_cleaningMemory && !_clearingHistory && _latencyCancellation == null && _throughputCancellation == null && _dnsCancellation == null;
            GpuSelector.IsEnabled = GpuRefreshButton.IsEnabled = GpuMinutes.IsEnabled = GpuLimit.IsEnabled = available;
            GpuStartButton.IsEnabled = available && GpuSelector.SelectedItem is GpuStressChoice { CanStress: true };
        }
    
        private async void RunGpuStress(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!GpuStartButton.IsEnabled || GpuSelector.SelectedItem is not GpuStressChoice { CanStress: true } choice || choice.Device.PciAddress == null) return;
            string pci = choice.Device.PciAddress;
            double limit = (double)(GpuLimit.Value ?? 80);
            var duration = TimeSpan.FromMinutes((double)(GpuMinutes.Value ?? 1));
            _gpuDurationSeconds = duration.TotalSeconds;
            GpuResultText.Text = $"Preparando {choice.Device.Name}; verificando novamente GPU e temperatura…";
            try
            {
                await RunAsync("gpu-stress", $"Stress GPU · {choice.Device.Name}", ct =>
                {
                    var result = VulkanGpuRenderer.Run(pci, duration, limit, ct, p => Volatile.Write(ref _gpuProgress, p), choice.Device);
                    return new BenchmarkMeasurement($"{result.Device} · {result.Frames} frames em {result.Seconds:N1} s. Não comprova estabilidade.",
                        null, new() { ["frames"] = result.Frames, ["seconds"] = result.Seconds, ["nonBlackPixels"] = result.NonBlackPixels });
                }, new() { ["gpuName"] = choice.Device.Name, ["gpuType"] = choice.Device.Kind, ["gpuPci"] = pci,
                    ["durationSeconds"] = duration.TotalSeconds.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) }, gpuThermalLimit: limit);
            }
            finally { Volatile.Write(ref _gpuProgress, null); }
        }
    }
    using System.Diagnostics;
    using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
    using Silk.NET.Vulkan;
    using VkBuffer = Silk.NET.Vulkan.Buffer;
    
    namespace ResetExplode.Hardware;
    
    public sealed record RenderGpu(string Name, string? PciAddress, uint VendorId, bool Hardware)
    {
        public string Kind { get; init; } = "Tipo não informado";
    }
    public sealed record GpuRenderProgress(long Frames, double Seconds, double Temperature);
    public sealed record GpuRenderResult(string Device, string PciAddress, long Frames, double Seconds, int NonBlackPixels);
    
    /// <summary>Pipeline gráfico Vulkan 1.1 offscreen, uso serial. Uma submissão por vez.</summary>
    public sealed unsafe class VulkanGpuRenderer : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly Vk _vk;
        private Instance _instance;
        private Device _device;
        private PhysicalDevice _physical;
        private Queue _queue;
        private Image _image;
        private DeviceMemory _memory;
        private ImageView _view;
        private RenderPass _pass;
        private Framebuffer _framebuffer;
        private PipelineLayout _layout;
        private Pipeline _pipeline;
        private CommandPool _pool;
        private CommandBuffer _command;
        private Fence _fence;
        private bool _disposed;
        private readonly uint _width, _height, _shells;
        public RenderGpu Selected { get; private set; } = null!;
    
        private static void Check(Result result)
        {
            if (result != Result.Success) throw new InvalidOperationException($"Vulkan: {result}.");
        }
    
        private VulkanGpuRenderer()
        {
            if (!OperatingSystem.IsLinux()) throw new PlatformNotSupportedException("Backend disponível apenas no Linux.");
            _vk = Vk.GetApi();
            try
            {
                ApplicationInfo app = new() { SType = StructureType.ApplicationInfo, ApiVersion = Vk.Version11 };
                InstanceCreateInfo info = new() { SType = StructureType.InstanceCreateInfo, PApplicationInfo = &app };
                Check(_vk.CreateInstance(in info, null, out _instance));
            }
            catch { _vk.Dispose(); throw; }
        }
    
        public static IReadOnlyList<RenderGpu> Enumerate()
        {
            using var renderer = new VulkanGpuRenderer();
            return renderer.Devices().Select(x => x.Info).ToArray();
        }
    
        private List<(PhysicalDevice Device, RenderGpu Info)> Devices()
        {
            uint count = 0;
            Check(_vk.EnumeratePhysicalDevices(_instance, ref count, null));
            var devices = new PhysicalDevice[count];
            fixed (PhysicalDevice* data = devices) Check(_vk.EnumeratePhysicalDevices(_instance, ref count, data));
            var result = new List<(PhysicalDevice, RenderGpu)>();
            foreach (var device in devices.Take((int)count))
            {
                _vk.GetPhysicalDeviceProperties(device, out var basic);
                string name = Marshal.PtrToStringUTF8((nint)basic.DeviceName) ?? "GPU sem nome";
                uint extCount = 0;
                Check(_vk.EnumerateDeviceExtensionProperties(device, (byte*)null, ref extCount, null));
                var extensions = new ExtensionProperties[extCount];
                fixed (ExtensionProperties* data = extensions)
                    Check(_vk.EnumerateDeviceExtensionProperties(device, (byte*)null, ref extCount, data));
                bool pciSupported = false;
                foreach (var extension in extensions)
                {
                    var copy = extension;
                    if (Marshal.PtrToStringUTF8((nint)copy.ExtensionName) == "VK_EXT_pci_bus_info") pciSupported = true;
                }
                string? address = null;
                if (pciSupported && basic.ApiVersion >= Vk.Version11)
                {
                    PhysicalDevicePCIBusInfoPropertiesEXT pci = new() { SType = StructureType.PhysicalDevicePciBusInfoPropertiesExt };
                    PhysicalDeviceProperties2 properties = new() { SType = StructureType.PhysicalDeviceProperties2, PNext = &pci };
                    _vk.GetPhysicalDeviceProperties2(device, &properties);
                    if (pci.PciDomain <= 65535 && pci.PciBus <= 255 && pci.PciDevice <= 31 && pci.PciFunction <= 7)
                        address = $"{pci.PciDomain:x4}:{pci.PciBus:x2}:{pci.PciDevice:x2}.{pci.PciFunction:x1}";
                }
                result.Add((device, new(name, address, basic.VendorID,
                    basic.DeviceType is PhysicalDeviceType.DiscreteGpu or PhysicalDeviceType.IntegratedGpu)
                    { Kind = basic.DeviceType switch { PhysicalDeviceType.IntegratedGpu => "Integrada", PhysicalDeviceType.DiscreteGpu => "Dedicada",
                        PhysicalDeviceType.Cpu => "Software", PhysicalDeviceType.VirtualGpu => "Virtual", _ => "Outro" } }));
            }
            return result;
        }
    
        private VulkanGpuRenderer(string pciAddress, uint width, uint height, uint shells) : this()
        {
            _width = width; _height = height; _shells = shells;
            try
            {
                var matches = Devices().Where(x => x.Info.PciAddress == pciAddress && x.Info.Hardware).ToArray();
                if (matches.Length != 1) throw new NotSupportedException("GPU PCI ausente, ambígua ou sem identificação física Vulkan.");
                (_physical, Selected) = matches[0];
                _vk.GetPhysicalDeviceProperties(_physical, out var properties);
                if (properties.ApiVersion < Vk.Version11) throw new NotSupportedException("Vulkan 1.1 é obrigatório.");
                uint count = 0;
                _vk.GetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(_physical, ref count, null);
                var families = new QueueFamilyProperties[count];
                fixed (QueueFamilyProperties* data = families) _vk.GetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(_physical, ref count, data);
                int family = Array.FindIndex(families, f => f.QueueCount > 0 && (f.QueueFlags & QueueFlags.GraphicsBit) != 0);
                if (family < 0) throw new NotSupportedException("Fila gráfica indisponível.");
                float priority = 1;
                DeviceQueueCreateInfo queue = new() { SType = StructureType.DeviceQueueCreateInfo, QueueFamilyIndex = (uint)family, QueueCount = 1, PQueuePriorities = &priority };
                DeviceCreateInfo deviceInfo = new() { SType = StructureType.DeviceCreateInfo, QueueCreateInfoCount = 1, PQueueCreateInfos = &queue };
                Check(_vk.CreateDevice(_physical, in deviceInfo, null, out _device));
                _vk.GetDeviceQueue(_device, (uint)family, 0, out _queue);
                CreateTarget();
                CreatePipeline();
                CommandPoolCreateInfo pool = new() { SType = StructureType.CommandPoolCreateInfo, QueueFamilyIndex = (uint)family, Flags = CommandPoolCreateFlags.ResetCommandBufferBit };
                Check(_vk.CreateCommandPool(_device, in pool, null, out _pool));
                CommandBufferAllocateInfo allocate = new() { SType = StructureType.CommandBufferAllocateInfo, CommandPool = _pool, Level = CommandBufferLevel.Primary, CommandBufferCount = 1 };
                Check(_vk.AllocateCommandBuffers(_device, in allocate, out _command));
                FenceCreateInfo fence = new() { SType = StructureType.FenceCreateInfo };
                Check(_vk.CreateFence(_device, in fence, null, out _fence));
            }
            catch { Dispose(); throw; }
        }
    
        private void CreateTarget()
        {
            _vk.GetPhysicalDeviceFormatProperties(_physical, Format.R8G8B8A8Unorm, out var format);
            var required = FormatFeatureFlags.ColorAttachmentBit | FormatFeatureFlags.ColorAttachmentBlendBit | FormatFeatureFlags.TransferSrcBit;
            if ((format.OptimalTilingFeatures & required) != required)
                throw new NotSupportedException("Formato RGBA8 não suporta render target.");
            ImageCreateInfo image = new() { SType = StructureType.ImageCreateInfo, ImageType = ImageType.Type2D,
                Format = Format.R8G8B8A8Unorm, Extent = new(_width, _height, 1), MipLevels = 1, ArrayLayers = 1,
                Samples = SampleCountFlags.Count1Bit, Tiling = ImageTiling.Optimal, Usage = ImageUsageFlags.ColorAttachmentBit | ImageUsageFlags.TransferSrcBit,
                SharingMode = SharingMode.Exclusive, InitialLayout = ImageLayout.Undefined };
            Check(_vk.CreateImage(_device, in image, null, out _image));
            _vk.GetImageMemoryRequirements(_device, _image, out var requirements);
            _vk.GetPhysicalDeviceMemoryProperties(_physical, out var memory);
            uint? type = null;
            for (uint i = 0; i < memory.MemoryTypeCount; i++)
                if ((requirements.MemoryTypeBits & (1u << (int)i)) != 0 && (memory.MemoryTypes[(int)i].PropertyFlags & MemoryPropertyFlags.DeviceLocalBit) != 0) { type = i; break; }
            if (type == null) throw new NotSupportedException("Memória local para render target indisponível.");
            MemoryAllocateInfo allocation = new() { SType = StructureType.MemoryAllocateInfo, AllocationSize = requirements.Size, MemoryTypeIndex = type.Value };
            Check(_vk.AllocateMemory(_device, in allocation, null, out _memory));
            Check(_vk.BindImageMemory(_device, _image, _memory, 0));
            ImageViewCreateInfo view = new() { SType = StructureType.ImageViewCreateInfo, Image = _image,
                ViewType = ImageViewType.Type2D, Format = image.Format, SubresourceRange = new(ImageAspectFlags.ColorBit, 0, 1, 0, 1) };
            Check(_vk.CreateImageView(_device, in view, null, out _view));
            AttachmentDescription attachment = new() { Format = image.Format, Samples = SampleCountFlags.Count1Bit,
                LoadOp = AttachmentLoadOp.Clear, StoreOp = AttachmentStoreOp.Store, StencilLoadOp = AttachmentLoadOp.DontCare,
                StencilStoreOp = AttachmentStoreOp.DontCare, InitialLayout = ImageLayout.Undefined, FinalLayout = ImageLayout.ColorAttachmentOptimal };
            AttachmentReference reference = new(0, ImageLayout.ColorAttachmentOptimal);
            SubpassDescription subpass = new() { PipelineBindPoint = PipelineBindPoint.Graphics, ColorAttachmentCount = 1, PColorAttachments = &reference };
            SubpassDependency dependency = new() { SrcSubpass = Vk.SubpassExternal, DstSubpass = 0,
                SrcStageMask = PipelineStageFlags.ColorAttachmentOutputBit, DstStageMask = PipelineStageFlags.ColorAttachmentOutputBit,
                SrcAccessMask = AccessFlags.ColorAttachmentWriteBit, DstAccessMask = AccessFlags.ColorAttachmentReadBit | AccessFlags.ColorAttachmentWriteBit };
            RenderPassCreateInfo pass = new() { SType = StructureType.RenderPassCreateInfo, AttachmentCount = 1, PAttachments = &attachment,
                SubpassCount = 1, PSubpasses = &subpass, DependencyCount = 1, PDependencies = &dependency };
            Check(_vk.CreateRenderPass(_device, in pass, null, out _pass));
            var target = _view;
            FramebufferCreateInfo framebuffer = new() { SType = StructureType.FramebufferCreateInfo, RenderPass = _pass,
                AttachmentCount = 1, PAttachments = &target, Width = _width, Height = _height, Layers = 1 };
            Check(_vk.CreateFramebuffer(_device, in framebuffer, null, out _framebuffer));
        }
    
        private ShaderModule Shader(string suffix)
        {
            var assembly = typeof(VulkanGpuRenderer).Assembly;
            using var stream = assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(assembly.GetManifestResourceNames().Single(n => n.EndsWith(suffix, StringComparison.Ordinal)))!;
            byte[] bytes = new byte[stream.Length]; stream.ReadExactly(bytes);
            fixed (byte* code = bytes)
            {
                ShaderModuleCreateInfo info = new() { SType = StructureType.ShaderModuleCreateInfo, CodeSize = (nuint)bytes.Length, PCode = (uint*)code };
                Check(_vk.CreateShaderModule(_device, in info, null, out var shader));
                return shader;
            }
        }
    
        private void CreatePipeline()
        {
            ShaderModule vertex = default, fragment = default;
            try
            {
                vertex = Shader("burn.vert.spv"); fragment = Shader("burn.frag.spv");
                byte* entry = stackalloc byte[] { 109, 97, 105, 110, 0 };
                var stages = stackalloc PipelineShaderStageCreateInfo[2];
                stages[0] = new() { SType = StructureType.PipelineShaderStageCreateInfo, Stage = ShaderStageFlags.VertexBit, Module = vertex, PName = entry };
                stages[1] = new() { SType = StructureType.PipelineShaderStageCreateInfo, Stage = ShaderStageFlags.FragmentBit, Module = fragment, PName = entry };
                PipelineVertexInputStateCreateInfo input = new() { SType = StructureType.PipelineVertexInputStateCreateInfo };
                PipelineInputAssemblyStateCreateInfo assembly = new() { SType = StructureType.PipelineInputAssemblyStateCreateInfo, Topology = PrimitiveTopology.TriangleList };
                Viewport viewport = new(0, 0, _width, _height, 0, 1);
                Rect2D scissor = new(new(0, 0), new(_width, _height));
                PipelineViewportStateCreateInfo view = new() { SType = StructureType.PipelineViewportStateCreateInfo, ViewportCount = 1, PViewports = &viewport, ScissorCount = 1, PScissors = &scissor };
                PipelineRasterizationStateCreateInfo raster = new() { SType = StructureType.PipelineRasterizationStateCreateInfo, PolygonMode = PolygonMode.Fill, CullMode = CullModeFlags.None, FrontFace = FrontFace.CounterClockwise, LineWidth = 1 };
                PipelineMultisampleStateCreateInfo samples = new() { SType = StructureType.PipelineMultisampleStateCreateInfo, RasterizationSamples = SampleCountFlags.Count1Bit };
                PipelineColorBlendAttachmentState blend = new() { BlendEnable = true, SrcColorBlendFactor = BlendFactor.SrcAlpha,
                    DstColorBlendFactor = BlendFactor.OneMinusSrcAlpha, ColorBlendOp = BlendOp.Add, SrcAlphaBlendFactor = BlendFactor.One,
                    DstAlphaBlendFactor = BlendFactor.Zero, AlphaBlendOp = BlendOp.Add,
                    ColorWriteMask = ColorComponentFlags.RBit | ColorComponentFlags.GBit | ColorComponentFlags.BBit | ColorComponentFlags.ABit };
                PipelineColorBlendStateCreateInfo blending = new() { SType = StructureType.PipelineColorBlendStateCreateInfo, AttachmentCount = 1, PAttachments = &blend };
                PushConstantRange range = new(ShaderStageFlags.VertexBit | ShaderStageFlags.FragmentBit, 0, 16);
                PipelineLayoutCreateInfo layout = new() { SType = StructureType.PipelineLayoutCreateInfo, PushConstantRangeCount = 1, PPushConstantRanges = &range };
                Check(_vk.CreatePipelineLayout(_device, in layout, null, out _layout));
                GraphicsPipelineCreateInfo pipeline = new() { SType = StructureType.GraphicsPipelineCreateInfo, StageCount = 2, PStages = stages,
                    PVertexInputState = &input, PInputAssemblyState = &assembly, PViewportState = &view, PRasterizationState = &raster,
                    PMultisampleState = &samples, PColorBlendState = &blending, Layout = _layout, RenderPass = _pass, Subpass = 0 };
                Check(_vk.CreateGraphicsPipelines(_device, default, 1, in pipeline, null, out _pipeline));
            }
            finally
            {
                if (fragment.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyShaderModule(_device, fragment, null);
                if (vertex.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyShaderModule(_device, vertex, null);
            }
        }
    
        private void Draw(float seconds, Action observe, Action submitted)
        {
            Check(_vk.ResetCommandBuffer(_command, 0));
            CommandBufferBeginInfo begin = new() { SType = StructureType.CommandBufferBeginInfo, Flags = CommandBufferUsageFlags.OneTimeSubmitBit };
            Check(_vk.BeginCommandBuffer(_command, in begin));
            ClearValue clear = new() { Color = new ClearColorValue(0, 0, 0, 1) };
            RenderPassBeginInfo pass = new() { SType = StructureType.RenderPassBeginInfo, RenderPass = _pass, Framebuffer = _framebuffer,
                RenderArea = new(new(0, 0), new(_width, _height)), ClearValueCount = 1, PClearValues = &clear };
            _vk.CmdBeginRenderPass(_command, in pass, SubpassContents.Inline);
            _vk.CmdBindPipeline(_command, PipelineBindPoint.Graphics, _pipeline);
            float* constants = stackalloc float[] { seconds, (float)_width / _height, _shells, 32 };
            _vk.CmdPushConstants(_command, _layout, ShaderStageFlags.VertexBit | ShaderStageFlags.FragmentBit, 0, 16, constants);
            _vk.CmdDraw(_command, 160 * 64 * 6, _shells, 0, 0);
            _vk.CmdEndRenderPass(_command);
            Check(_vk.EndCommandBuffer(_command));
            Check(_vk.ResetFences(_device, 1, in _fence));
            var command = _command;
            SubmitInfo submit = new() { SType = StructureType.SubmitInfo, CommandBufferCount = 1, PCommandBuffers = &command };
            observe(); // Checagem imediatamente antes de submeter trabalho.
            Check(_vk.QueueSubmit(_queue, 1, in submit, _fence));
            submitted();
            Exception? stopped = null;
            // Não liberar recursos em uso pela GPU. Cancelamento impede novas submissões,
            // mas ainda espera a fence do frame já enviado (driver travado pode atrasar o retorno).
            while (true)
            {
                var result = _vk.WaitForFences(_device, 1, in _fence, true, 100_000_000);
                if (stopped == null) { try { observe(); } catch (Exception ex) { stopped = ex; } }
                if (result == Result.Timeout) continue;
                Check(result); break;
            }
            if (stopped != null) System.Runtime.ExceptionServices.ExceptionDispatchInfo.Capture(stopped).Throw();
        }
    
        private int VerifyPixels()
        {
            VkBuffer buffer = default;
            DeviceMemory memory = default;
            try
            {
                ulong size = (ulong)_width * _height * 4;
                BufferCreateInfo bufferInfo = new() { SType = StructureType.BufferCreateInfo, Size = size,
                    Usage = BufferUsageFlags.TransferDstBit, SharingMode = SharingMode.Exclusive };
                Check(_vk.CreateBuffer(_device, in bufferInfo, null, out buffer));
                _vk.GetBufferMemoryRequirements(_device, buffer, out var requirements);
                _vk.GetPhysicalDeviceMemoryProperties(_physical, out var properties);
                uint? type = null;
                var flags = MemoryPropertyFlags.HostVisibleBit | MemoryPropertyFlags.HostCoherentBit;
                for (uint i = 0; i < properties.MemoryTypeCount; i++)
                    if ((requirements.MemoryTypeBits & (1u << (int)i)) != 0 && (properties.MemoryTypes[(int)i].PropertyFlags & flags) == flags)
                    { type = i; break; }
                if (type == null) throw new NotSupportedException("Memória de leitura dos pixels indisponível.");
                MemoryAllocateInfo allocation = new() { SType = StructureType.MemoryAllocateInfo, AllocationSize = requirements.Size, MemoryTypeIndex = type.Value };
                Check(_vk.AllocateMemory(_device, in allocation, null, out memory));
                Check(_vk.BindBufferMemory(_device, buffer, memory, 0));
                Check(_vk.ResetCommandBuffer(_command, 0));
                CommandBufferBeginInfo begin = new() { SType = StructureType.CommandBufferBeginInfo, Flags = CommandBufferUsageFlags.OneTimeSubmitBit };
                Check(_vk.BeginCommandBuffer(_command, in begin));
                ImageMemoryBarrier barrier = new() { SType = StructureType.ImageMemoryBarrier,
                    SrcAccessMask = AccessFlags.ColorAttachmentWriteBit, DstAccessMask = AccessFlags.TransferReadBit,
                    OldLayout = ImageLayout.ColorAttachmentOptimal, NewLayout = ImageLayout.TransferSrcOptimal,
                    SrcQueueFamilyIndex = Vk.QueueFamilyIgnored, DstQueueFamilyIndex = Vk.QueueFamilyIgnored,
                    Image = _image, SubresourceRange = new(ImageAspectFlags.ColorBit, 0, 1, 0, 1) };
                _vk.CmdPipelineBarrier(_command, PipelineStageFlags.ColorAttachmentOutputBit, PipelineStageFlags.TransferBit,
                    0, 0, null, 0, null, 1, &barrier);
                BufferImageCopy copy = new() { ImageSubresource = new(ImageAspectFlags.ColorBit, 0, 0, 1), ImageExtent = new(_width, _height, 1) };
                _vk.CmdCopyImageToBuffer(_command, _image, ImageLayout.TransferSrcOptimal, buffer, 1, in copy);
                MemoryBarrier host = new() { SType = StructureType.MemoryBarrier,
                    SrcAccessMask = AccessFlags.TransferWriteBit, DstAccessMask = AccessFlags.HostReadBit };
                _vk.CmdPipelineBarrier(_command, PipelineStageFlags.TransferBit, PipelineStageFlags.HostBit,
                    0, 1, &host, 0, null, 0, null);
                Check(_vk.EndCommandBuffer(_command));
                Check(_vk.ResetFences(_device, 1, in _fence));
                var command = _command;
                SubmitInfo submit = new() { SType = StructureType.SubmitInfo, CommandBufferCount = 1, PCommandBuffers = &command };
                Check(_vk.QueueSubmit(_queue, 1, in submit, _fence));
                Check(_vk.WaitForFences(_device, 1, in _fence, true, ulong.MaxValue));
                void* data = null;
                Check(_vk.MapMemory(_device, memory, 0, size, 0, &data));
                try
                {
                    var pixels = new ReadOnlySpan<byte>(data, checked((int)size));
                    int count = 0;
                    for (int i = 0; i < pixels.Length; i += 4)
                        if (pixels[i] != 0 || pixels[i + 1] != 0 || pixels[i + 2] != 0) count++;
                    return count;
                }
                finally { _vk.UnmapMemory(_device, memory); }
            }
            finally
            {
                // Também necessário se ocorrer falha após a submissão da cópia.
                _vk.DeviceWaitIdle(_device);
                if (buffer.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyBuffer(_device, buffer, null);
                if (memory.Handle != 0) _vk.FreeMemory(_device, memory, null);
            }
        }
    
        public static GpuRenderResult Run(string pciAddress, TimeSpan duration, double thermalLimit = 80,
            CancellationToken cancellation = default, Action<GpuRenderProgress>? progress = null, RenderGpu? expectedDevice = null)
        {
            if (duration < TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100) || duration > TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5))
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(duration));
            var guard = new GpuThermalGuard(pciAddress, thermalLimit);
            cancellation.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
            var devices = Enumerate().Where(d => d.PciAddress == pciAddress && d.Hardware).ToArray();
            if (devices.Length != 1) throw new NotSupportedException("GPU selecionada ausente ou ambígua; atualize a lista.");
            if (expectedDevice != null && devices[0] != expectedDevice)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("A GPU mudou desde a seleção; atualize a lista antes de iniciar.");
            using var sensor = new LinuxGpuTemperatureSource(new LinuxFiles());
            guard.Check(sensor.Read(devices[0]));
            using var renderer = new VulkanGpuRenderer(pciAddress, 640, 360, 32);
            if (renderer.Selected.Name != devices[0].Name || renderer.Selected.VendorId != devices[0].VendorId)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Identificação da GPU mudou; atualize a lista.");
            var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            long frames = 0;
            bool submitted = false;
            double lastTemperature = 0;
            void Observe()
            {
                cancellation.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                var reading = sensor.Read(renderer.Selected);
                try { guard.Check(reading); }
                catch (GpuThermalException ex) { ex.LoadStarted = submitted; throw; }
                lastTemperature = reading.Celsius!.Value;
            }
            while (clock.Elapsed < duration)
            {
                Observe();
                renderer.Draw((float)clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, Observe, () => submitted = true);
                frames++;
                progress?.Invoke(new(frames, clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, lastTemperature));
            }
            Observe();
            if (frames == 0) throw new InvalidOperationException("Prazo encerrado sem frame concluído.");
            double seconds = clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds;
            int pixels = renderer.VerifyPixels();
            Observe();
            if (pixels == 0) throw new InvalidOperationException("O pipeline não produziu pixels visíveis.");
            return new(renderer.Selected.Name, pciAddress, frames, seconds, pixels);
        }
    
        public void Dispose()
        {
            if (_disposed) return;
            _disposed = true;
            if (_device.Handle != 0)
            {
                _vk.DeviceWaitIdle(_device);
                if (_fence.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyFence(_device, _fence, null);
                if (_pool.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyCommandPool(_device, _pool, null);
                if (_pipeline.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyPipeline(_device, _pipeline, null);
                if (_layout.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyPipelineLayout(_device, _layout, null);
                if (_framebuffer.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyFramebuffer(_device, _framebuffer, null);
                if (_pass.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyRenderPass(_device, _pass, null);
                if (_view.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyImageView(_device, _view, null);
                if (_image.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyImage(_device, _image, null);
                if (_memory.Handle != 0) _vk.FreeMemory(_device, _memory, null);
                _vk.DestroyDevice(_device, null);
            }
            if (_instance.Handle != 0) _vk.DestroyInstance(_instance, null);
            _vk.Dispose();
        }
    }
